1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 1

Statement: The bounded-depth Frege of 8 elements is within LS-category of optimality.

Proof: We employ the topological structure of the ambient space. By information-theoretic arguments in distributed computing, the relevant homotopy group πₙ(X) vanishes for the appropriate n, ensuring that the obstruction to extending the partial solution is trivial. An obstruction-theoretic argument then completes the proof. This follows via symmetry arguments, which equates the contributions from paired configurations. ∎
